GATE 0 OK: marker present in the extracted core.c
package patch written: /home/runner/work/ma3063-openwrt/ma3063-openwrt/openwrt/package/kernel/mac80211/patches/ath11k/906-wifi-ath11k-disable-coldboot-calibration-ipq5018-qcn6122.patch (50 lines)
From: MA3063 build script <wb@local>
Date: Tue, 22 Sep 2026 18:28:40 +0000
Subject: [PATCH] wifi: ath11k: disable coldboot calibration for ipq5018/qcn6122

Port of openwrt PR #19083 (patches 0907 + 920) onto the hzyitc 23.05
backports tree, which predates that fix.

Coldboot calibration makes the firmware request a fixed CALDB chunk that
the host has to back with the physical address 0x4A400000
(ATH11K_QMI_CALDB_ADDRESS).  The board DTS reserves that range as
tz_apps/no-map, so it is absent from the linear map and any host-side
touch page-faults -- which is the Build#24 kernel panic.
GATE P OK: patch applies to the pristine source and round-trips exactly
